Text: # SRC-001: Cents per kilometre method / work-related care expenses

SOURCE_ID: SRC-001
SOURCE_TITLE: Cents per kilometre method / work-related car expenses
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/api/public/content/0-13e18f54-38a8-447a-84ce-4f830352fdfd
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
CLAIMS_SUPPORTED: T01, T04, T05
SOURCE_ORGANISATION: Australian Taxation Office

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-001-CHUNK-01
SOURCE_ID: SRC-001
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/api/public/content/0-13e18f54-38a8-447a-84ce-4f830352fdfd
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Cents per kilometre method

CONTENT: 
The cents per kilometre method allows a deduction  based on eligible work-related kilometres travelled.

The method has a maximum of 5,000 work-related kilometres per car for the income year.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-001-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-001
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/api/public/content/0-13e18f54-38a8-447a-84ce-4f830352fdfd
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Records and calculation

CONTENT:
The taxpayer must be able to demonstrate how the number of work-related kilometres was calculated.

Individual receipts for each car expense are not required when using the cents per kilometre method.

SOURCE_ID: SRC-010

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-010-CHUNK-01
SOURCE_ID: SRC-010
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/individuals-and-families/income-deductions-offsets-and-records/deductions-you-can-claim/work-related-deductions/education-training-and-seminars/self-education-expenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Connection to current employment

CONTENT:
Self-education expenses can be deductible where the study maintains or improves skills or knowledge used in the taxpayer's current employment, or is likely to increase income from those current activities.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-010-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-010
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/individuals-and-families/income-deductions-offsets-and-records/deductions-you-can-claim/work-related-deductions/education-training-and-seminars/self-education-expenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: New employment

CONTENT:
Study expenses are not deductible where the education is undertaken to obtain new employment or change to a different type of employment.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-010-CHUNK-03
SOURCE_ID: SRC-010
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/individuals-and-families/income-deductions-offsets-and-records/deductions-you-can-claim/work-related-deductions/education-training-and-seminars/self-education-expenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Assets over $300

CONTENT:
For an eligible depreciating asset used for work-related self-education, an asset costing more than $300 is generally claimed through decline in value over its effective life rather than as an immediate full deduction.

RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Additional running expenses

CONTENT:
A worker may claim additional running expenses caused by working from home, such as eligible energy, internet, phone and depreciating-asset costs.

Private household use is not deductible.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-004-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-004
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/api/public/content/0-b7755b71-423a-413f-8ea9-0cf06cf2857a
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Occupancy expenses

CONTENT:
Rent or mortgage-interest type occupancy expenses are only potentially deductible in limited circumstances, such as where part of the home qualifies as a place of business.

Ordinary occasional working from home does not automatically make rent deductible.

SOURCE_ID: SRC-002
SOURCE_TITLE: Work-related travel expenses
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/myTax25WorkRelatedTravelExpenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
CLAIMS_SUPPORTED: T02, T15, T24
SOURCE_ORGANISATION: Australian Taxation Office

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-002-CHUNK-01
SOURCE_ID: SRC-002
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/myTax25WorkRelatedTravelExpenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: General travel deduction rule

CONTENT:
An employee can claim work-related travel expenses they incur in the course of performing their work, provided the expense is directly related to earning income and is not reimbursed.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-002-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-002
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/myTax25WorkRelatedTravelExpenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Private travel

CONTENT:
Normal travel between home and the regular workplace is generally private and not deductible.

Food and incidental expenses are also generally not deductible where the worker does not sleep away from home overnight.
Attributes: {}

RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Deductible gift recipient requirement

A tax deduction is generally available only where the gift or donation is made to an organisation with deductible gift recipient status and the other gift conditions are met.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-011-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-011
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/individuals-and-families/income-deductions-offsets-and-records/deductions-you-can-claim/gifts-and-donations
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Crowdfunding

CONTENT:
Payments made through crowdfunding or social-media fundraising are not automatically deductible.

Deductibility depends on whether the recipient is an eligible deductible gift recipient and the payment satisfies the gift rules.

Text: # SRC-012: $300 work-related expense rule and record-keeping exception

SOURCE_ID: SRC-012
SOURCE_TITLE: $300 work-related expense rule and record-keeping exception
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/law/view/document?LocID=%22TXR%2FTR20201%2FNAT%2FATO%2FatH37%22&PiT=20251112000001
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
CLAIMS_SUPPORTED: T07, T18
SOURCE_ORGANISATION: Australian Taxation Office

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-012-CHUNK-01
SOURCE_ID: SRC-012
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/law/view/document?LocID=%22TXR%2FTR20201%2FNAT%2FATO%2FatH37%22&PiT=20251112000001
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: $300 work related expense threshold

CONTENT:
The $300 rule is a substantiation exception, not a deduction by itself.

The taxpayer must actually incur deductible expenses and must still be able to explain how the claim was calculated.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-012-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-012
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/law/view/document?LocID=%22TXR%2FTR20201%2FNAT%2FATO%2FatH37%22&PiT=20251112000001
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Laundry up to $150

CONTENT:
A laundry claim of $150 or less can fall within a written-evidence exception, but the taxpayer still needs a reasonable basis or record showing how the amount was worked out.

CLAIMS_SUPPORTED: T17, T25
SOURCE_ORGANISATION: Australian Taxation Office

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-010-CHUNK-01
SOURCE_ID: SRC-010
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/individuals-and-families/income-deductions-offsets-and-records/deductions-you-can-claim/work-related-deductions/education-training-and-seminars/self-education-expenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Connection to current employment

CONTENT:
Self-education expenses can be deductible where the study maintains or improves skills or knowledge used in the taxpayer's current employment, or is likely to increase income from those current activities.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-010-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-010
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/individuals-and-families/income-deductions-offsets-and-records/deductions-you-can-claim/work-related-deductions/education-training-and-seminars/self-education-expenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: New employment

CONTENT:
Study expenses are not deductible where the education is undertaken to obtain new employment or change to a different type of employment.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-010-CHUNK-03
SOURCE_ID: SRC-010
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/individuals-and-families/income-deductions-offsets-and-records/deductions-you-can-claim/work-related-deductions/education-training-and-seminars/self-education-expenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Assets over $300

CONTENT:
For an eligible depreciating asset used for work-related self-education, an asset costing more than $300 is generally claimed through decline in value over its effective life rather than as an immediate full deduction.

Private household use is not deductible.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-004-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-004
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/api/public/content/0-b7755b71-423a-413f-8ea9-0cf06cf2857a

SECTION: Occupancy expenses

CONTENT:
Rent or mortgage-interest type occupancy expenses are only potentially deductible in limited circumstances, such as where part of the home qualifies as a place of business.

Ordinary occasional working from home does not automatically make rent deductible.

SOURCE_ORGANISATION: Australian Taxation Office

CHUNK_ID: SRC-001-CHUNK-01
SOURCE_ID: SRC-001
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/api/public/content/0-13e18f54-38a8-447a-84ce-4f830352fdfd
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Cents per kilometre method

CONTENT: 
The cents per kilometre method allows a deduction  based on eligible work-related kilometres travelled.

The method has a maximum of 5,000 work-related kilometres per car for the income year.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-001-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-001
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/api/public/content/0-13e18f54-38a8-447a-84ce-4f830352fdfd
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Records and calculation

CONTENT:
The taxpayer must be able to demonstrate how the number of work-related kilometres was calculated.

Individual receipts for each car expense are not required when using the cents per kilometre method.

Rent or mortgage-interest type occupancy expenses are only potentially deductible in limited circumstances, such as where part of the home qualifies as a place of business.

Ordinary occasional working from home does not automatically make rent deductible.

Any private use needs to be excluded from the claim.

SOURCE_ID: SRC-003
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/api/public/content/0-f4168ea2-73dc-4bb7-9fa4-9468254b86b1
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Conventional clothing

CONTENT:
Everyday or conventional clothing is not deductible merely because an employer requires it or it is only worn at work.

Deductions may be available for eligible compulsory uniforms, occupation-specific clothing and protective clothing.

SOURCE_ID: SRC-003

RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Driver licence

CONTENT:
The ordinary cost of obtaining or renewing a driver's licence is private, even where driving is required for work.

Additional costs for certain special licences or conditions needed to perform employment duties may be deductible.

The taxpayer must actually incur deductible expenses and must still be able to explain how the claim was calculated.

SECTION: Laundry up to $150

SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/individuals-and-families/income-deductions-offsets-and-records/deductions-you-can-claim/work-related-deductions/education-training-and-seminars/self-education-expenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Connection to current employment

CONTENT:
Self-education expenses can be deductible where the study maintains or improves skills or knowledge used in the taxpayer's current employment, or is likely to increase income from those current activities.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-010-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-010
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/individuals-and-families/income-deductions-offsets-and-records/deductions-you-can-claim/work-related-deductions/education-training-and-seminars/self-education-expenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: New employment

CONTENT:
Study expenses are not deductible where the education is undertaken to obtain new employment or change to a different type of employment.

---

SOURCE_ID: SRC-010

RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Assets over $300

CONTENT:
For an eligible depreciating asset used for work-related self-education, an asset costing more than $300 is generally claimed through decline in value over its effective life rather than as an immediate full deduction.

CONTENT:

SECTION: $300 work related expense threshold

CONTENT:
The $300 rule is a substantiation exception, not a deduction by itself.

The taxpayer must actually incur deductible expenses and must still be able to explain how the claim was calculated.

---

SOURCE_ID: SRC-012
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/law/view/document?LocID=%22TXR%2FTR20201%2FNAT%2FATO%2FatH37%22&PiT=20251112000001
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Laundry up to $150

CONTENT:
A laundry claim of $150 or less can fall within a written-evidence exception, but the taxpayer still needs a reasonable basis or record showing how the amount was worked out.

Rent or mortgage-interest type occupancy expenses are only potentially deductible in limited circumstances, such as where part of the home qualifies as a place of business.

Individual receipts for each car expense are not required when using the cents per kilometre method.

CONTENT:
Study expenses are not deductible where the education is undertaken to obtain new employment or change to a different type of employment.

SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/individuals-and-families/income-deductions-offsets-and-records/deductions-you-can-claim/work-related-deductions/education-training-and-seminars/self-education-expenses

SOURCE_ID: SRC-012
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/law/view/document?LocID=%22TXR%2FTR20201%2FNAT%2FATO%2FatH37%22&PiT=20251112000001
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: $300 work related expense threshold

CONTENT:
The $300 rule is a substantiation exception, not a deduction by itself.

The taxpayer must actually incur deductible expenses and must still be able to explain how the claim was calculated.

---

RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Laundry up to $150

CONTENT:
A laundry claim of $150 or less can fall within a written-evidence exception, but the taxpayer still needs a reasonable basis or record showing how the amount was worked out.

RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: General travel deduction rule

CONTENT:

CONTENT:
Normal travel between home and the regular workplace is generally private and not deductible.

RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Additional running expenses

CONTENT:
A worker may claim additional running expenses caused by working from home, such as eligible energy, internet, phone and depreciating-asset costs.

Private household use is not deductible.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-004-CHUNK-02

RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Occupancy expenses

CONTENT:
Rent or mortgage-interest type occupancy expenses are only potentially deductible in limited circumstances, such as where part of the home qualifies as a place of business.

Ordinary occasional working from home does not automatically make rent deductible.

Text: # SRC-004 — Working from home expenses

SOURCE_ID: SRC-004
SOURCE_TITLE: Working from home expenses
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/api/public/content/0-b7755b71-423a-413f-8ea9-0cf06cf2857a
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
CLAIMS_SUPPORTED: T09, T28
SOURCE_ORGANISATION: Australian Taxation Office

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-004-CHUNK-01
SOURCE_ID: SRC-004
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/api/public/content/0-b7755b71-423a-413f-8ea9-0cf06cf2857a
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Additional running expenses

CONTENT:
A worker may claim additional running expenses caused by working from home, such as eligible energy, internet, phone and depreciating-asset costs.

Private household use is not deductible.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-004-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-004
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/api/public/content/0-b7755b71-423a-413f-8ea9-0cf06cf2857a
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Occupancy expenses

CONTENT:
Rent or mortgage-interest type occupancy expenses are only potentially deductible in limited circumstances, such as where part of the home qualifies as a place of business.

Ordinary occasional working from home does not automatically make rent deductible.
Attributes: {}

CHUNK_ID: SRC-003-CHUNK-01
SOURCE_ID: SRC-003
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/api/public/content/0-f4168ea2-73dc-4bb7-9fa4-9468254b86b1
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Conventional clothing

CONTENT:
Everyday or conventional clothing is not deductible merely because an employer requires it or it is only worn at work.

Deductions may be available for eligible compulsory uniforms, occupation-specific clothing and protective clothing.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-003-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-003
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/api/public/content/0-f4168ea2-73dc-4bb7-9fa4-9468254b86b1
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Driver licence

CONTENT:
The ordinary cost of obtaining or renewing a driver's licence is private, even where driving is required for work.

Additional costs for certain special licences or conditions needed to perform employment duties may be deductible.

Text: # SRC-002 — Work-related travel expenses

SOURCE_ID: SRC-002
SOURCE_TITLE: Work-related travel expenses
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/myTax25WorkRelatedTravelExpenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
CLAIMS_SUPPORTED: T02, T15, T24
SOURCE_ORGANISATION: Australian Taxation Office

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-002-CHUNK-01
SOURCE_ID: SRC-002
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/myTax25WorkRelatedTravelExpenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: General travel deduction rule

CONTENT:
An employee can claim work-related travel expenses they incur in the course of performing their work, provided the expense is directly related to earning income and is not reimbursed.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-002-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-002
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/myTax25WorkRelatedTravelExpenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Private travel

CONTENT:
Normal travel between home and the regular workplace is generally private and not deductible.

Food and incidental expenses are also generally not deductible where the worker does not sleep away from home overnight.
Attributes: {}

RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Deductible gift recipient requirement

CONTENT:
A tax deduction is generally available only where the gift or donation is made to an organisation with deductible gift recipient status and the other gift conditions are met.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-011-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-011

RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Crowdfunding

CONTENT:
Payments made through crowdfunding or social-media fundraising are not automatically deductible.

Deductibility depends on whether the recipient is an eligible deductible gift recipient and the payment satisfies the gift rules.

Text: # SRC-001: Cents per kilometre method / work-related care expenses

SOURCE_ID: SRC-001
SOURCE_TITLE: Cents per kilometre method / work-related car expenses
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/api/public/content/0-13e18f54-38a8-447a-84ce-4f830352fdfd
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
CLAIMS_SUPPORTED: T01, T04, T05
SOURCE_ORGANISATION: Australian Taxation Office

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-001-CHUNK-01
SOURCE_ID: SRC-001
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/api/public/content/0-13e18f54-38a8-447a-84ce-4f830352fdfd
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Cents per kilometre method

CONTENT: 
The cents per kilometre method allows a deduction  based on eligible work-related kilometres travelled.

The method has a maximum of 5,000 work-related kilometres per car for the income year.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-001-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-001
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/api/public/content/0-13e18f54-38a8-447a-84ce-4f830352fdfd
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Records and calculation

CONTENT:
The taxpayer must be able to demonstrate how the number of work-related kilometres was calculated.

Individual receipts for each car expense are not required when using the cents per kilometre method.

Text: # SRC-012: $300 work-related expense rule and record-keeping exception

SOURCE_ID: SRC-012
SOURCE_TITLE: $300 work-related expense rule and record-keeping exception
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/law/view/document?LocID=%22TXR%2FTR20201%2FNAT%2FATO%2FatH37%22&PiT=20251112000001
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
CLAIMS_SUPPORTED: T07, T18
SOURCE_ORGANISATION: Australian Taxation Office

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-012-CHUNK-01
SOURCE_ID: SRC-012
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/law/view/document?LocID=%22TXR%2FTR20201%2FNAT%2FATO%2FatH37%22&PiT=20251112000001
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: $300 work related expense threshold

CONTENT:
The $300 rule is a substantiation exception, not a deduction by itself.

The taxpayer must actually incur deductible expenses and must still be able to explain how the claim was calculated.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-012-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-012
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/law/view/document?LocID=%22TXR%2FTR20201%2FNAT%2FATO%2FatH37%22&PiT=20251112000001
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Laundry up to $150

CONTENT:
A laundry claim of $150 or less can fall within a written-evidence exception, but the taxpayer still needs a reasonable basis or record showing how the amount was worked out.

Text: # SRC-010 — Self-education expenses

SOURCE_ID: SRC-010
SOURCE_TITLE: Self-education expenses
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/individuals-and-families/income-deductions-offsets-and-records/deductions-you-can-claim/work-related-deductions/education-training-and-seminars/self-education-expenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
CLAIMS_SUPPORTED: T17, T25
SOURCE_ORGANISATION: Australian Taxation Office

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-010-CHUNK-01
SOURCE_ID: SRC-010
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/individuals-and-families/income-deductions-offsets-and-records/deductions-you-can-claim/work-related-deductions/education-training-and-seminars/self-education-expenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Connection to current employment

CONTENT:
Self-education expenses can be deductible where the study maintains or improves skills or knowledge used in the taxpayer's current employment, or is likely to increase income from those current activities.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-010-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-010
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/individuals-and-families/income-deductions-offsets-and-records/deductions-you-can-claim/work-related-deductions/education-training-and-seminars/self-education-expenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: New employment

CONTENT:
Study expenses are not deductible where the education is undertaken to obtain new employment or change to a different type of employment.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-010-CHUNK-03
SOURCE_ID: SRC-010
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/individuals-and-families/income-deductions-offsets-and-records/deductions-you-can-claim/work-related-deductions/education-training-and-seminars/self-education-expenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Assets over $300

CONTENT:
For an eligible depreciating asset used for work-related self-education, an asset costing more than $300 is generally claimed through decline in value over its effective life rather than as an immediate full deduction.

SECTION: Depreciating assets

An immediate deduction may be available for an eligible asset costing $300 or less where the relevant tests are met.

More expensive eligible assets are generally claimed through decline in value.

SOURCE_ORGANISATION: Australian Taxation Office

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-007-CHUNK-01
SOURCE_ID: SRC-007
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/api/public/content/0-4aea1f9c-507a-4474-9fb8-46c2cc1c2822
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Gym and fitness expenses

CONTENT:
Gym fees and fitness-related expenses are generally private and not deductible.

Limited exceptions may apply where the required physical activity is sufficiently connected with the employee's duties.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-007-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-007
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/api/public/content/0-4aea1f9c-507a-4474-9fb8-46c2cc1c2822
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Sun protection

CONTENT:
Eligible sun-protection products may be deductible where the employee is exposed to the sun for prolonged periods while performing work duties.

Any private use needs to be excluded from the claim.

Text: # SRC-010 — Self-education expenses

SOURCE_ID: SRC-010
SOURCE_TITLE: Self-education expenses
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/individuals-and-families/income-deductions-offsets-and-records/deductions-you-can-claim/work-related-deductions/education-training-and-seminars/self-education-expenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
CLAIMS_SUPPORTED: T17, T25
SOURCE_ORGANISATION: Australian Taxation Office

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-010-CHUNK-01
SOURCE_ID: SRC-010
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/individuals-and-families/income-deductions-offsets-and-records/deductions-you-can-claim/work-related-deductions/education-training-and-seminars/self-education-expenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Connection to current employment

CONTENT:
Self-education expenses can be deductible where the study maintains or improves skills or knowledge used in the taxpayer's current employment, or is likely to increase income from those current activities.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-010-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-010
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/individuals-and-families/income-deductions-offsets-and-records/deductions-you-can-claim/work-related-deductions/education-training-and-seminars/self-education-expenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: New employment

CONTENT:
Study expenses are not deductible where the education is undertaken to obtain new employment or change to a different type of employment.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-010-CHUNK-03
SOURCE_ID: SRC-010
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/individuals-and-families/income-deductions-offsets-and-records/deductions-you-can-claim/work-related-deductions/education-training-and-seminars/self-education-expenses
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Assets over $300

CONTENT:
For an eligible depreciating asset used for work-related self-education, an asset costing more than $300 is generally claimed through decline in value over its effective life rather than as an immediate full deduction.

Text: # SRC-012: $300 work-related expense rule and record-keeping exception

SOURCE_ID: SRC-012
SOURCE_TITLE: $300 work-related expense rule and record-keeping exception
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/law/view/document?LocID=%22TXR%2FTR20201%2FNAT%2FATO%2FatH37%22&PiT=20251112000001
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
CLAIMS_SUPPORTED: T07, T18
SOURCE_ORGANISATION: Australian Taxation Office

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-012-CHUNK-01
SOURCE_ID: SRC-012
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/law/view/document?LocID=%22TXR%2FTR20201%2FNAT%2FATO%2FatH37%22&PiT=20251112000001
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: $300 work related expense threshold

CONTENT:
The $300 rule is a substantiation exception, not a deduction by itself.

The taxpayer must actually incur deductible expenses and must still be able to explain how the claim was calculated.

---

CHUNK_ID: SRC-012-CHUNK-02
SOURCE_ID: SRC-012
SOURCE_URL: https://www.ato.gov.au/law/view/document?LocID=%22TXR%2FTR20201%2FNAT%2FATO%2FatH37%22&PiT=20251112000001
RETRIEVAL_DATE: 2026-10-10
SECTION: Laundry up to $150

CONTENT:
A laundry claim of $150 or less can fall within a written-evidence exception, but the taxpayer still needs a reasonable basis or record showing how the amount was worked out.
